iḷantiyar eṉpatu irupāṟ potup paṉmip peyar  (Young men or Women or both)
iḷanti = iḷami
makara meyyīṟṟu iyaṟpeyari (proper name) uyarvup paṉmiyiṟ kuṟittal vēṇṭiṉ,  ṉakara meyyīṟṟup peyarāka māṟṟikkoḷḷalām.
e-ṭu : 
peyar	uyarvup paṉmi		paṉmi
celvam - celvaṉ celvaṉār 	celvar, celvaṉmār
mēṟkāṭṭiya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu potuvāka ar īṟu paṉmiyiyum,  ār īṟu uyarvup paṉmiyiyum  uṇart tum eṉa aṟintukoḷka.
kumaraṉ eṉṉum teṉcol kum eṉṉum aṭippiṟantu, kūṭṭattiṟkut takuntavaṉ allatu tiraṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil iḷiñaṉiyē kuṟittatu. itaṉ peṇpāl vaṭivāṉa kumari eṉṉum collum itē poruḷil iḷiñiyik kuṟittatu.
kummutal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal. kum - kummal = kuviyal. 
kum - kumi - kuvi - kuvi, kuvāl, kuvivu, kuvavu.
kumi - kumiyal, - kuviyal. kummiruṭṭu - tiṇinta kāriruḷ.
kuvavuttōḷ = tiraṇṭa tōḷ.
kum - kumpu - kumpal = kūṭṭam. kumputal = kūṭutal.
kumpu = kūṭṭam.
kum - kumar, tiraṇṭa iḷami, iḷami, kaṉṉimi, 
aḻiyāttaṉmi. kumar - kumaraṉ, kumari.
kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa murukaṉum pālinilat teyvamāṉa kāḷiyum, eṉṟum iḷamiyar eṉṉuṅ karuttup paṟṟiyē muṟiyē, kumaraṉ kumari eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kāḷiyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭut teṉkōṭi maliyum vaṭakōṭiyāṟum kumariyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. kumarimaliyiṉ peyarālēyē mūḻkip pōṉa teṉkaṇṭattik kumarikkaṇṭam eṉkiṉṟōm. kumarimaliyirunta kālam, āriyar eṉṟa iṉamē tōṉṟāta toṉmutu paṇṭik  kālamākum.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar  tamiḻiṉ  toṉmiyi muṟṟum maṟittut tamiḻi vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vaḻiyatākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, piṉvarumāṟu  kumaraṉ kumariyeṉṉum  iru coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭiviyum poruḷiyum tirintum mikuttum uḷḷaṉar.
kumaraṉ - kumāra = kuḻanti, piyaṉ , iḷiñaṉ , makaṉ
						( irukkuvētam)
kumari - kumārī - ciṟumi. pattiliruntu paṉṉīrakavippaṭṭavaḷ. 		iḷiñi, makaḷ (atarvavētam).
iv virucoṟkaḷiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭumāṟu,   mūlattiyum kīḻvarumāṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar.
ku + māra = eḷitāka iṟappatu.
ip pakuppum coṟporuṭ karaṇiyamum iyaṟkikku māṟākavum uttikkup poruntāmalum iruppatiyum, makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷ tamiḻiliṉmiyiyum nōkkuka. iṉṟum, `intac cumiyit tūkka muṭiyāta nī oru kumaraṉā? eṉṟu ōr iḷiñaṉi nōkki makkaḷ viṉavuvatiyum, kōṭic cēlikku oru veḷḷi, kumarip peṇṇukku oru piḷḷi eṉṉum paḻamoḻi vaḻakkiyum, ūṉṟi nōkki uṇmiyi aṟika.
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum coṟkaḷi vaṭacolleṉṟu nīkkiviṭiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟi ottukkoṇṭatākavum kumarimali mūḻkiyatu āriya varukikkup piṟpaṭṭatākavumē muṭital kāṇka.
tiru eṉṉum colliṉ pala poruḷkaḷuḷ teyvat taṉmi eṉpatum oṉṟu .
e-ṭu :  tiruk kaṇṇappar, tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvaraṅkam, tiruviḻā, tirunīṟu, tirumaṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷil `tiru' eṉpatu teyvattaṉmik karuttōṭu tūymik karuttiyum uṇarttum.
matippāṉa makkaṭṭaṉmiyik kuṟikkum tiruvāḷaṉ eṉṉum aṭiccol, tiru. eṉṟu kuṟukiniṟkumpōtu muṟṟup puḷḷi peṟa vēṇṭum. allākkāl, teyvattaṉmiyuṇarttum tiru eṉṉum collō ṭoppakkoṇṭu mayaṅka nērum.
e-ṭu :   tirunāvukkaracu  (iṟiyaṭiyār peyar)
	  tiru. nāvukkaracu (potumakaṉ peyar)
iṟiyaṭiyār peyarē, potumakaṉ peyarāyiṉ appaṭiyē yirukkalām. puḷḷi vēṇṭiyatilli. tuṟaviyār peyarukku muṉ tavattiru eṉpatiyum, tamiḻt toṇṭar peyarukku muṉ tamiḻttiru eṉpatiyum, maṟiyoḻukkattiṉar peyarukku muṉ maṟittiru eṉpatiyum, aṭiccollāka ,āḷalām.
e-ṭu :	tavattiruk kuṉṟakkuṭiyaṭikaḷ
	tamiḻttiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
	maṟittiru maṇiyam avarkaḷ
srī la srī eṉṉum civamaṭa vaḻakkit tiruttavattiru allatu peruntavattiru eṉṟu kuṟikkalām.
tirumati eṉṉum aṭiccol, tiru eṉṉum teṉ colliyum mati eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyīṟṟut tiripiyuṅ koṇṭa irupiṟappi (hybrid) ātalāl, ati aṟavē vilakkalvēṇṭum.
tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ - srīmat (va.) - srīmatī (peṇpāl)
tirumati - srīmati. tiruvāṭṭi eṉṉum tūya teṉcolli nīkkiviṭṭut tirumati eṉṉum irupiṟappiyi āḷvatu,
	pētimi yeṉpa tiyāteṉiṉ ētaṅkoṇ
  	 ṭūtiyam pōka viṭal			       (kuṟaḷ. 831)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkē eṭuttukkāṭṭām. orukāl, tirumati eṉpataṉ īṟṟi aṟivup pulaṉik kuṟikkum teṉcollākak karutikkoṇṭaṉar pōlum! aṟivuttiṟaṉik kuṟitkum mati eṉṉum teṉcol vēṟu; peṇpāluṇarttum matī eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyīṟu vēṟu.
iṉi, matippaṭic coṟkaḷ. (1) muṉṉaṭiccoṟkaḷ, (2) piṉṉaṭic coṟkaḷ eṉa iru vakiya.
kaṇṇiyam vāyntavar peyarukkuppiṉ avarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippatu piṉṉaṭic collākum. atu uyarvup paṉmi. ataṟku orumiyum paṉmiyum illi.
e-ṭu : tiru. māṇikkavēl ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ. 
āṟṟalum tērvuppaṭṭamum
pulamiyum toḻilum kuṟittuvarum coṟkaḷ muṉṉaṭiyāka variṉ vēṟaṭi tēviyilli.

pulavar pukaḻēntiyār, pukaḻēntip pulavar.
periyār ī. vē. irā. ī. vē. irāp periyār
`avarkaḷ` eṉṉum piṉṉaṭiyi evar peyarkkum piṉ kuṟikkalām. atu eḻutuvāriṉ nilimiyiyum viruppattiyum poṟuttatu.
araciṉar vaḻaṅkum paṭṭaṅkaḷāṉa aṭiccoṟkaḷip piṉvarumāṟu moḻipeyarkkalām:
patmasrī:  tāmarit tiru. va. cuppiyāp piḷḷi avarkaḷ
cer (Sir) : vayavar tiyākarācac ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ
